# Non-Google LookML corpus candidates

Generated 2026-10-08 06:25 UTC by `make_report.py` from `pool.json` (GitHub search metadata) + `measured.json` (grep/regex measurements of shallow clones under `~/work/corpus/<owner>__<repo>`) + `google_overlap.json` (file/view-name overlap against 219 looker-open-source/llooker LookML repos cloned into `_google_blocks/`). No LookML parser was run.

Pool: 1996 distinct repos returned by the searches; 1782 cloned+measured (every non-excluded repo ≤200 MB API size). Classification counts: candidate=156, demo_copy=295, derivative_of_corpus_repo=1, google_block_copy=282, no_lkml=71, no_model=94, no_refinement_or_extends=860, synthetic=2, tool_fixture=21.

Shortlist rule: not a tool fixture / synthetic / Google-block copy / demo copy, ≥1 `*.model.lkml`, ≥1 refinement (`view:/explore: +name`) or `extends:`. Tier 1 = organisation-owned real projects/blocks; Tier 2 = tool-vendor demo projects; Tier 3 = individual sandboxes (ranked last; only the top 15 by score are shown here, the rest are in B). Score = 3·refinements + extends + lkml/10 + 5·models (capped).
